### Coding Slice 2026-06-13 (DM1 V1 chest open object stack-split source-locked gate)

- ✅ 2026-06-13 — added `test_dm1_v1_chest_open_stack_split_pc34_compat`, a contract-only DM1 V1 chest open object stack-split source-locked gate for the `dm1_v1_auto_mechanics_object_stack_split_merge_gate` lane. The named lane is exactly the object stack split/merge pair: the F0334 "merge" close path is already covered by `test_dm1_v1_chest_close_stack_merge_pc34_compat`, and the F0333 "split" open path was the nearest uncovered DM1 V1 edge in the same subsystem. The gate pins the ReDMCSB `CHEST.C F0333:30-67` chain walk via DUNGEON.C F0159 from the `P0693_ps_Container->Slot` chain head through the 8-item cap break at `L1019_i_ThingCount > 8` (lines 62-65), the C0xFFFF_THING_NONE tail fill for G0425[visible_count..7] (lines 68-74), the C0xFFFE_THING_ENDOFLIST walk-stop condition, and the M569_PANEL_CHEST = 6 panel-content transition at line 28. It asserts: (a) the empty chain open produces 0 visible items + 8 NONE tail fills and the panel content reaches M569_PANEL_CHEST; (b) the partial chain (length 5) open produces 5 visible items in chain order + 3 NONE tail fills; (c) the exact chain (length 8) open produces 8 visible items + 0 tail fills; (d) the overfull chain (length 12) open produces 8 visible items (V1..V8) and the 8th item's `Next` field still points to the 9th item (the hidden tail), reachable via F0159 from the 8th item, with the 9th, 10th, 11th, 12th items in chain order and the 12th terminated by C0xFFFE_THING_ENDOFLIST; (e) the F0333 / F0334 round-trip identity: open(overfull) + close(merge) + reopen(split) preserves the chain head, body order, and hidden tail, with the F0163 list-append walk-hop count equal to the visible_count - 1 = 7; (f) the F0333 open path does not call F0163 (no relink) or F0334 (no rewire); (g) the M569_PANEL_CHEST panel content is stable across open + close + reopen; (h) the F0333 split is idempotent on the visible window + chain head + hidden tail when the same chest is opened twice. ReDMCSB anchors: `CHEST.C F0333:30-67`, `CHEST.C F0334:79-130`, `DUNGEON.C F0159:1664-1681`, `DUNGEON.C F0163:1769-1838`, `DUNGEON.C F0140:1114-1120`, `DEFS.H:3005-3008`. Non-duplicative: the F0334 merge half is fully covered by `test_dm1_v1_chest_close_stack_merge_pc34_compat` (the disjoint F0163 call-pattern + leader-hand-independence + reopen-round-trip on a sparse 6-item close with a leader hand holding a same-kind stackable scroll); the F0333 split-side first-eight materialization + hidden-tail behavior is already covered separately by `test_dm1_v1_chest_open_visible_slots_pc34_compat` and `test_dm1_v1_chest_ninth_item_hidden_tail_pc34_compat`, but the lane adds the (i) chain head vs hidden-tail read invariant (F0333 line 60 chain-head read + F0159 walk via GENERIC->Next), (ii) the F0333/F0334 round-trip identity on a 12-item overfull chain, (iii) the chain-tail reachability from the 8th item's Next, and (iv) the F0333 split idempotence — all disjoint contracts. Source-locked contract-only marker; no real-asset or original-DOS pixel parity claim. Local verification: `cmake --build builds/n2-build --target test_dm1_v1_chest_open_stack_split_pc34_compat --parallel 4` builds clean (0 warnings), the strict `cc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -O2` build of both the source and test files is also clean, the test runs 119/119 assertions, `ctest -R "dm1_v1_chest_close_stack_merge|dm1_v1_chest_open_visible_slots|dm1_v1_chest_ninth_item_hidden_tail|dm1_v1_chest_open_stack_split"` passes 4/4 (the new stack-split + the existing merge, visible-slots, ninth-tail family all green), the related `dm1_v1_chest_` ctest bucket still passes 71/71 (no regression), the related `dm1_v1_mirror_candidate_` ctest bucket still passes 70/70 (no regression), `SDL_VIDEODRIVER=dummy ./builds/n2-build/firestaff_m11_phase_a_probe` passes 23/23, and `git diff --check` is clean. The new files are tracked: `include/firestaff/dm1/v1/chest/open_stack_split_pc34_compat.h` (force-added, .gitignore `firestaff` line matches `include/firestaff/`, same pre-existing pattern that bit pass768/783/786/788/789/790plus/797/C045), `src/dm1/dm1_v1_chest_open_stack_split_pc34_compat.c`, and `tests/test_dm1_v1_chest_open_stack_split_pc34_compat.c`. The follow-up adds (a) a `settle_only` step type and `settle_s` per-step dwell in `PlanStep` (DM.EXE SELECTOR text menus — graphics/sound/input + entrance-wall — do not fit the 4-state dungeon density classifier, so they now send keys, dwell, capture, and proceed, with only the entrance ENTER click and the dungeon hold classifier-gated); and (b) an `OSASCRIPT_KEY_CODES` table + `_osascript_key_code` AppleScript-System-Events key-code path in `_press_key` (`cliclick kp:return` / `t:1` were silently dropped by DOSBox even with the window frontmost, the SELECTOR echoed the typed digit but never consumed the Return, so the live route now sends route keys via `osascript -e 'tell application "System Events" to key code N'` with a re-raise before each keystroke, falling back to `keystroke` for single characters not in the explicit key-code map). The follow-up is intentional and non-duplicative with the 10:34 retune (which only adjusted the plan shape and the `title_screen → entrance_wall` step); it carries the empirical fix for the remaining two host-input gaps. ReDMCSB-derived routing for the route anchors is unchanged from the 10:34 entry — the SELECTOR `1. VGA / 2. EGA / 3. Tandy Q. Quit` text page is a DOS-internal selector, not a DUNVIEW.C DUNGEONVIEW_Draw_CPSF framebuffer, and the route's `enter_dungeon` click target `(696/799, 160/599)` on the right-hand stone wall is the empirical DM PC 3.4 ENTER button from the original `01_ingame_start.png` capture. Local verification: `python3 -B docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_session.py --dry-run` 6/6 state-machine transitions, `python3 -B tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` 19/19 runbook/tool consistency, `python3 -B tools/verify_dm1_v1_original_capture_live_row_gate.py` 8/8 live row gate, `SDL_VIDEODRIVER=dummy ./build/firestaff_m11_phase_a_probe` 23/23 invariants, `git diff --check` clean. The evidence note records the first reproducible live run that actually reaches `dungeon_gameplay` and writes capture artifacts, with a capture receipt of 6 stable samples at `viewport_nonblack=0.9268, rightcol_nonblack=0.1154 (< 0.135), full_nonblack=0.4647`. No proprietary game frames are committed; only hashes/densities and route facts. The follow-up is local-only on this watchdog session and is being pushed to origin/main; the watchdog will watch the new GitHub Actions run.

### Opus worker 2026-06-13 (DM1 V1 original DOS capture reaches dungeon_gameplay, capture-backed)

- ✅ 2026-06-13 — the DM1 PC 3.4 original-DOS `--live` route in `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_session.py` now reaches the real in-game dungeon viewport (`dungeon_gameplay`) and writes capture artifacts, reproduced across two back-to-back runs on DOSBox Staging 0.82.2 / macOS 15 with the `dosbox-rawshot` backend. The 10:34 watchdog retune got the plan shape right but had only been `--dry-run` verified; live runs still stalled because of three host-input facts isolated here with executable probes: (1) **window focus** — the inner `Contents/MacOS/dosbox` subprocess stayed behind Terminal and AppleScript `set frontmost` did not raise it, so `_activate_dosbox` now goes through `open -a` first (`_open_activate_dosbox`); (2) **key delivery** — cliclick `kp:return`/`t:1` were silently dropped by DOSBox even with the window frontmost (the SELECTOR echoed the typed digit but never consumed Return), so `_press_key` now sends route keys via AppleScript `key code N` (`OSASCRIPT_KEY_CODES`, `_osascript_key_code`) with a re-raise before each keystroke; (3) **SELECTOR classification** — the DM.EXE graphics/sound/input text menus do not fit the 4-state dungeon density classifier (graphics page ≈ `entrance_menu`, sound page ≈ `title_screen`), so those steps are now `settle_only` (send keys, dwell, capture, proceed) and only the entrance ENTER click and the final dungeon hold are classifier-gated. Verified live receipt: 6 stable `dungeon_gameplay` samples at viewport_nonblack=0.9268, rightcol_nonblack=0.1154 (< 0.135), full_nonblack=0.4647, normalized RGB sha256 `3a5ab1a8edd2e5a8…`, `PASS live captures written`. Self-tests held: `--dry-run` 6/6 PASS, `test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` 19/19 PASS, `dosbox_state_detector.py --self-test` 5/5 PASS, route-handoff 6/6, live-row-gate 8/8, `git diff --check` clean. No proprietary game frames are committed; the route facts and hash/density receipt are recorded in `parity-evidence/dm1_v1_original_capture_dungeon_entry_reached.md`. Known follow-up (not claimed here): in-dungeon forward movement uses DM PC's on-screen mouse controls, not the arrow keys, so the post-entry step-forward capture does not yet show movement.

Anchors: dosbox-capture-session `_trigger_dosbox_internal_screenshot` (Alt+F5 + option modifier, the only screenshot key macOS 15 doesn't intercept as a system keyboard-navigation shortcut — Cmd+F5 and Ctrl+F5 are both swallowed; paired with `glshader=none` + `default_image_capture_formats=rendered raw` in `_write_live_conf` so the rendered capture is a clean square-pixel framebuffer without CRT shader overlay); `_open_activate_dosbox` + `MACOS_DOSBOX_OPEN_APP_NAMES = ("DOSBox Staging", "DOSBox")` (raises the DOSBox window above Terminal via `open -a` because AppleScript `set frontmost` does NOT raise a window launched as a bare `Contents/MacOS/dosbox` subprocess, so every cliclick/osascript keystroke was being delivered to Terminal and DOSBox never saw Ctrl+F5 or the route keys); new `DEFAULT_PLAN` (graphics_select 1/Return → sound_select 1/Return → input_select 1/Return → entrance_wall → enter_dungeon with `entrance_enter_click` pseudo-key (cliclick at framebuffer fractional (696/799, 160/599) on the right-hand stone wall) → dungeon_gameplay classifier v≈0.93, r≈0.12 < 0.135); the legacy never-arriving `title_screen` step (DM.EXE boots straight into the SELECTOR's *graphics* text menu "1.[*]VGA 2.[*]EGA 3.[ ]Tandy Q.[*]Quit" with no black title screen first — that selector frame classifies as `entrance_menu` v≈0.38 / r≈0.21, NOT `title_screen`) is removed, and the plan length drops from 7 to 6 steps. Paired test refactor: `_default_plan_length(capture_session)` (importlib-loader that reads the live `DEFAULT_PLAN` length without hard-coding 8/8, so a future deliberate plan-length change updates the gate automatically while a real classifier/plan regression that drops a transition still fails). ReDMCSB anchors: `CHAMPION.C F0284:93-130 F0284_CHAMPION_SetPartyDirection` (the per-champion Cell + Direction rotation, G0308 update, F0296 redraw), `CHAMPION.C F0296_CHAMPION_DrawChangedObjectIcons` (the per-champion icon redraw that keeps C038 panel priority byte, C037 status hand box, C159 champion icon, M070 panel owner ordinal, and M568 panel content byte-stable), `REVIVE.C F0282:744-806 F0282_CHAMPION_ProcessCommands160To162_ClickInResurrectReincarnatePanel` (the C160/C161/C162 close-click path that reads `M516_CHAMPIONS[G0305_ui_PartyChampionCount - 1]` on the post-shuffle party and clears `G0299_ui_CandidateChampionOrdinal`), `COMMAND.C F0361:1709-1813` (keyboard turn input queue write), `COMMAND.C F0359:1452-1662` (C040 panel Yes click queue write), `COMMAND.C F0380:2045-2156` (single-command drain), and `DEFS.H` anchors `C040`, `C037`, `C038`, `C159`, `C160`, `C161`, `C162`, `M070`, `M516`. The gate covers (1) the room-transition plan for a STAIRS trigger returns `preserveChampionInventories=1`, `preserveChampionStats=1`, `preserveLeaderHandObject=1`, `mapChanged=1`, `requestSetCurrentMap=1`, `requestSetCurrentMapAndPartyMap=1`, (2) `m11_stairs_use` mutates the level state (`currentLevel`, `transitionActive`, `transitionFromLevel`, `transitionToLevel`, `transitionTicksLeft`) but the `M11_InventoryState` next to it stays byte-untouched: `G0426_T_OpenChest` keeps the open chest's thing ordinal, all 8 `G0425_aT_ChestSlots[i]` keep their itemType/weight byte-identical, `G0424_i_PanelContent` stays at `DM1_PC34_PANEL_CHEST`, the leader's `C01_SLOT_ACTION_HAND` keeps its lit-torch itemType/weight/charges, and `M516_CHAMPIONS[0].Load` is preserved at 45 (1 torch + 2+3+4+5+6+7+8+9 chest weights), (3) the same byte-stability holds across a second stairs step in the opposite direction (round-trip level 0→1→0), (4) `m11_stairs_tick` runs the transition to completion (16 × 100 ms ticks) and the inventory is still byte-stable, (5) when no chest is open, the panel content stays at `DM1_PC34_PANEL_INVENTORY` across a stairs step and 32 ticks, and (6) the same-pose degenerate case (`mapIndex` 0→0) returns `rc=1, active=0, mapChanged=0` so the planner is robust to a no-op stairs repeat. - ✅ 2026-06-12 07:40 — `release: prepare Firestaff v2.7.9 DM1 MacBook Pro launch fixes`. The v2.7.8 MacBook Pro report found two DM1 launch regressions: FTL/SWSH did not animate with the original cadence, and Entrance door buttons did not respond. The renderer now resolves SDL3 high-DPI resize events by keeping `SDL_GetWindowSize()` logical coordinates for mouse mapping while using `SDL_GetRenderOutputSize()` pixels for presentation, including `SDL_EVENT_WINDOW_PIXEL_SIZE_CHANGED`; this keeps ReDMCSB entrance button hit-tests in 320x200 source space on Retina displays. The SWSH runtime now uses the source-locked animation step table instead of a local hardcoded loop, batches adjacent `Setcolor()` writes into the next visible wait frame, and applies the ReDMCSB `DBF` wait semantics as N+1 VBlanks (30 effective palette VBlanks total). Focused verification passed: `test_m11_display_aspect_present_rect`, `test_swsh_frontend_pc34_compat_integration`, `firestaff_dm1_v1_entrance_button_click_runtime_probe` 17/17, Phase A 23/23, and `git diff --check`.

- ✅ 2026-06-13 — added `tools/verify_dm1_v1_original_capture_live_row_gate.py`, a hermetic DM1 V1 original-capture live row gate. The gate wires the on-disk live session runner's exact ``original/01_ingame_start.png`` + ``original/02_ingame_step_forward.png`` filenames (the files `live_run()` writes at lines 2124 / 2132 of `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_session.py`) to the pass623 canonical input-capture fixture and the Firestaff fixture viewport hash set, then runs the same on-disk preflight + row builder + transcript writer + pass608 verifier chain used by the existing handoff closure gate and confirms the pass608 same-viewport capture blocker flips from `BLOCKED_PASS608_DM1_V1_SAME_VIEWPORT_CAPTURE_NOT_PROMOTABLE` to `PASS608_DM1_V1_COMMAND_STATE_REDRAW_TRANSCRIPT_BOUND`. The gate is fully hermetic (8 sub-checks: importable pass608 verifier with BLOCKED baseline, preflight self-test, regex pin on the live session runner's `save()` filenames, anchored binding against the real on-disk pass623 fixture + the real on-disk Firestaff capture manifest, row builder renders a 41-column row per live binding, transcript writer emits a promotable transcript, pass608 flips to PROMOTED, and a negative path that patches the second row's Firestaff hash to a never-seen 64-hex value and confirms BLOCKED is preserved) and runs in CI as the CTest `dm1_v1_original_capture_live_row_gate` and as a sub-check of `tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` under `live_row_gate_selftest`. The live-binding table (`01_ingame_start.png` → `01_start_south_1_3` + sha `50661c78…8bf9`, `02_ingame_step_forward.png` → `03_blocked_west_wall_1_3` + sha `0cb83803…92b8`) is now part of the public contract; a future operator who renames the live capture file, the pass623 label, or the Firestaff fixture viewport sha gets a self-describing CI failure from the C3 / C4 / C6 / C7 / C8 sub-checks, so the next live DOSBox attempt cannot ship a transcript whose `originalFrame.path` is no longer in the live runbook. The slice is non-duplicative with the existing `verify_dm1_v1_original_capture_route_handoff.py` gate (which covers the standalone `02_turn_right_west_1_3` route, not the live runner's two `original/*.png` filenames), the events row builder / transcript writer / preflight self-tests, the `dm1_v1_creature_chain_original_capture_gate` creature-chain row pin, and the rewritten-same-name rawshot / focus-recovery / focus-mismatch failure-mode gates. Companion runbook step `Step 5e: Live Row Gate (deterministic)` documents the live-binding table and the 8 sub-checks. Local: `python3 tools/verify_dm1_v1_original_capture_live_row_gate.py` 8/8, `python3 tools/verify_dm1_v1_original_capture_route_handoff.py` 6/6 (no regression), `python3 tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` 19/19 (was 18/18, new `live_row_gate_selftest` adds the 19th), `git diff --check` clean. CMake CTest entry wired as `dm1_v1_original_capture_live_row_gate` with `TIMEOUT 300`. - ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route classifier hardening: `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_state_detector.py` now (a) divides per-pixel (not per-channel) so the 0.30 calibrated band matches the documented expectation, (b) drops the dead 0.70/0.10 envelope for a single calibrated 0.135 band, (c) ships a synthetic-fixture `--self-test` plus a corrected pass94 ground-truth table that reclassifies frames 05-06 as `dungeon_gameplay` (the runbook's "wall_closeup" label was itself produced by the same broken envelope), (d) fixes the `Image.LONEST` typo in `docs/parity/tools/compare_captures.py`, and (e) extracts the runbook's inline `dosbox_capture_session.py` into a real runnable scaffold at `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_session.py` with `--dry-run` that walks the state machine against the synth fixtures.  The two new CTest gates (`dm1_v1_original_capture_state_detector_self_test`, `dm1_v1_original_capture_session_dry_run`) catch classifier regressions in CI so the next DOSBox live attempt is no longer blocked by a buggy classifier.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route preflight gate: `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_preflight.py` runs the runbook §1 SHA256 checks (DUNGEON.DAT `d90b6b1c...`, GRAPHICS.DAT `2c3aa836...`) and writes a hardened `dosbox_capture.conf` whose settings are pinned to the runbook §2 values (machine=svga_s3, memsize=16, cpu core=dynamic, cycles=max, frameskip=0, windowresolution=1024x768, output=opengl).  The preflight refuses to write a conf that contains the historical pass94 failure-mode values (svga_paradise, memsize=4, core=normal, cycles=3000) and records a JSON receipt (`preflight.receipt.json`) with the verified SHA256s, the rendered settings, the chosen launch command (`DM.EXE`), and the current Firestaff git HEAD so the next capture-session manifest can cite the receipt.  CTest gate `dm1_v1_original_capture_preflight_self_test` exercises the matching case, the SHA-corruption case, the pass94 conf-pin-violation case, and the hardened-conf sanity case against hermetic synthetic fixtures (no real game data required).  This is the upstream gate for the runbook §2 "machine=svga_s3 is non-negotiable" requirement; the next live attempt that runs the preflight cannot reproduce the pass94 conf shape.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route runbook / tool handoff hardening: the runbook at `docs/parity/DM1_V1_ORIGINAL_CAPTURE_RUNBOOK.md` is now pinned to the on-disk capture tools by a new `tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` regression probe (CTest gate `dm1_v1_original_capture_runbook_consistency`).  The probe catches four classes of regressions that previously shipped through the runbook silently: (a) the trailing ``"Done"`` token on the Step 4 `convert` command that broke ImageMagick's output-path parse, (b) an inlined buggy draft of `compare_captures.py` (default NEAREST filter, no LANCZOS) that produced wildly wrong diffs for non-320x200 inputs, (c) an autoexec that pointed at `DungeonMasterPC34.EXE` (a directory on the canonical layout) instead of the actual binary `DM.EXE` inside that directory, and (d) a stale placeholder git hash in the Output Manifest Template.  The preflight at `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_preflight.py` now auto-detects the canonical game-root layout (DUNGEON.DAT + GRAPHICS.DAT in the root, `DungeonMasterPC34/DM.EXE` in the subdir) vs. the inner-subdir layout (DM.EXE directly under data_dir) and emits the right autoexec + launch_command in the receipt; the preflight's self-test now has 6 dedicated layout checks for the regression.  The runbook's inline `dosbox_capture_session.py` Step 3 autoexec is fixed to `cd DungeonMasterPC34` + `DM.EXE`, and the inline buggy `compare_captures.py` block is removed in favour of a reference to the on-disk tool.  The next live attempt that runs `tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` in CI cannot re-introduce any of these regressions.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route Output Manifest handoff code: a new `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_manifest_writer.py` (CTest gate `dm1_v1_original_capture_manifest_writer_self_test`, 14/14 hermetic self-test checks) turns the preflight receipt + a per-capture classifier output TSV into the runbook's Output Manifest Template with real SHA256s, a live `git rev-parse HEAD`, the receipt's pinned `launch_command` / `render_settings` / `session_id`, and the classifier's verdict for every capture.  The writer refuses to emit a manifest when the preflight pin checks are not all PASS, when a recorded SHA does not match the file on disk, when a classification is outside the runbook's documented state list (`dungeon_gameplay`, `entrance_menu`, `champion_create`, `title_screen`, `wall_closeup`, `unclassified`), or when the preflight receipt reports a `pass94_forbidden_present` failure.  The runbook at `docs/parity/DM1_V1_ORIGINAL_CAPTURE_RUNBOOK.md` now has a Step 5b "Render the Output Manifest (deterministic)" section that points operators at the on-disk writer and a "Do NOT fill this template in by hand" warning on the Output Manifest Template itself.  The runbook-consistency probe at `tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` now also runs the writer's `--self-test` and refuses to pass if the runbook stops referencing the on-disk tool, so a future operator cannot revert to hand-filling the manifest the way a previous draft shipped the stale `f7f3291f` placeholder hash.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route pass608 / pass625 runtime transcript handoff code: a new `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_transcript_writer.py` (CTest gate `dm1_v1_original_capture_transcript_writer_self_test`, 26/26 hermetic self-test checks) turns a per-capture events TSV + the preflight receipt + the pass623 canonical input-capture fixture + the Firestaff capture-manifest viewport-hash set into the ``transcript.json`` file the pass608 same-viewport capture blocker (``BLOCKED_PASS608_DM1_V1_SAME_VIEWPORT_CAPTURE_NOT_PROMOTABLE``) and the pass625 preflight (``FAIL_PASS625_DM1_V1_ORIGINAL_TRANSCRIPT_ROW_PREFLIGHT``) demand.  Every emitted row carries all 30 pass608 fields and all 40 pass625 fields (pass625 is a strict superset of pass608), the rows are mirrored under the three pass608 keys (``rows``/``transcriptRows``/``frameBindings``), and a top-level ``promotable`` flag tells downstream verifiers whether every row passed.  The writer refuses to emit a transcript when the preflight pin checks are not all PASS, when an input token has no ``sourceCommandId`` mapping in the pass623 fixture, when a row's command id is a TURN and ``dispatch.handler`` is the MOVE handler (or vice versa), when ``partyAfter`` does not match the F0128 redraw tuple, when ``firestaffFrame.viewportSha256`` is not in the canonical Firestaff ``viewport_224x136`` hash set, when a recorded ``originalFrame.rawSha256``/``cropSha256`` does not match the bytes on disk, when the events TSV header is re-ordered (the 41-column order is part of the public contract), or when the row's ``run_id`` is the pass625 template placeholder.  Every ReDMCSB source-function name (F0359/F0361, F0380, F0365/F0366, F0128, F0097/VIDRV_09_BlitViewPort) is pinned verbatim against the pass625 SOURCE_LOCKS list.  The runbook at `docs/parity/DM1_V1_ORIGINAL_CAPTURE_RUNBOOK.md` now has a Step 5c "Render the Runtime Transcript (deterministic)" section that points operators at the on-disk writer and explains how the next live DOSBox attempt can satisfy the pass608 ``runtimeTranscript.ok = true`` contract.  The runbook-consistency probe at `tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` now also runs the writer's `--self-test` and refuses to pass if the runbook stops referencing the on-disk tool, so a future operator cannot revert to hand-building the transcript the way the pass608 blocker stayed stuck on hand-built manifests.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route pass608 events TSV handoff code: a new `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_events_row_builder.py` (CTest gate `dm1_v1_original_capture_events_row_builder_self_test`, 12/12 hermetic self-test checks) turns one pass623 route label (e.g. `02_turn_right_west_1_3`) + a single 320x200 capture frame + a 224x136 crop into the 41-column events TSV row the transcript writer accepts.  Pulls every source-locked value (``F0380_COMMAND_ProcessQueue_CPSC``, ``F0365/F0366_COMMAND_ProcessTypes1To2/3To6``, ``F0128_DUNGEONVIEW_Draw_CPSF``, ``F0097_DUNGEONVIEW_DrawViewport``, ``VIDRV_09_BlitViewPort``, the runbook §1 ``GRAPHICS.DAT``/``DUNGEON.DAT`` SHA256s) and every pass623-fixture-pinned value (the ``inputToken`` → ``sourceCommandId`` mapping, the ``postTuple``, the Firestaff ``viewportSha256``) verbatim from the canonical contracts so the operator never has to type them.  For multi-command routes like `04_forward_south_1_4` (commands=[1, 3], tokens=[LEFT, UP]) the helper splits the route into one row per command, sharing the final postTuple and the pass623 viewport-hash.  The helper refuses to emit a row when the route label is not in the pass623 fixture, when the recorded 320x200 capture is missing or its on-disk SHA256 does not match, when the 224x136 crop is missing or its on-disk SHA256 does not match, when the preflight receipt's pin checks are not all PASS, or when the recorded ``inputToken``/``sourceCommandId`` pair does not match the pass623 fixture.  In the same pass, the writer's ``_load_pass623_fixture`` was extended to pair ``inputTokens`` with ``commandIds`` positionally for multi-command routes — a real pass623 fixture's `commands=[1, 3] / tokens=[LEFT, UP]` row now correctly pairs `LEFT → 1` and `UP → 3` (the legacy single-id mapping would have mapped both tokens to command 1 and broken the multi-command end-to-end binding).  The writer's `--self-test` was extended with a multi-command case (3 new checks, 26/26 → 29/29) and the runbook at `docs/parity/DM1_V1_ORIGINAL_CAPTURE_RUNBOOK.md` now has a Step 5d "Render the Events TSV (deterministic)" section that points operators at the on-disk helper.  The runbook-consistency probe at `tools/test_dm1_v1_capture_runbook_consistency.py` gained two more checks (13/13 → 15/15): a prose check that the runbook references the on-disk row builder and its `--self-test` gate, and a tool check that runs the helper's `--self-test` — so a future operator cannot revert to typing the 41 columns by hand the way a previous draft of the same-viewport route did.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture route handoff closure gate: a new `tools/verify_dm1_v1_original_capture_route_handoff.py` (CTest gate `dm1_v1_original_capture_route_handoff`, 6/6 hermetic closure checks) wires the on-disk `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_preflight.py` self-test, the on-disk `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_events_row_builder.py`, the on-disk `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_transcript_writer.py`, and the on-disk `tools/verify_pass608_dm1_v1_same_viewport_capture_blocker.py` into a single end-to-end check that the deterministic toolchain actually flips the pass608 same-viewport capture blocker from `BLOCKED_PASS608_DM1_V1_SAME_VIEWPORT_CAPTURE_NOT_PROMOTABLE` to `PASS608_DM1_V1_COMMAND_STATE_REDRAW_TRANSCRIPT_BOUND` with `runtimeTranscript.status == 'loaded_promotable_same_run'`.  Hermetic — synthesises a preflight receipt, a pass623 canonical input-capture fixture, a Firestaff capture manifest, and 320x200 / 224x136 capture fixtures in a temp dir, then runs the real on-disk tools as subprocesses and feeds the emitted `transcript.json` to the real pass608 verifier.  The negative-path subcheck patches `firestaffFrame.viewportSha256` to a never-seen 64-hex value and asserts the BLOCKED status is preserved with the `no supplied transcript row satisfies the pass608 command/state/redraw binding contract` blocker still present, so a future regression that drops the `firestaffViewportHashKnown` gate is caught at this gate instead of at the next live DOSBox attempt.  In the same pass, the pass608 verifier's `f0380SourceFunction` check was aligned with the ReDMCSB source chain (COMMAND.C:1452-1661 `F0359_COMMAND_ProcessClick_CPSC` queue write + COMMAND.C:1709-1813 `F0361_COMMAND_ProcessKeyPress` queue write + COMMAND.C:2045-2156 `F0380_COMMAND_ProcessQueue_CPSC` queue dispatch): the `commandQueue.sourceFunction` slot is now the queue *write* step (F0359/F0361, with F0380 also accepted for direct-dispatch routes) and a new `dispatchF0380SourceFunction` check pins the queue *dispatch* step on `dispatch.sourceFunction == F0380_COMMAND_ProcessQueue_CPSC`.  Both checks are cited in the verifier's comment with the ReDMCSB source-function / line range they pin.  The new gate is the regression guard that catches any future drift in the writer / verifier / source-chain contract before the next live attempt ships, and the next live attempt is no longer blocked by a misaligned pass608 source-function check.
- ✅ DM1 V1 original-capture live runner scaffold: `docs/parity/tools/dosbox_capture_session.py --live` now starts DOSBox Staging from a hardened live conf, sends the documented state-machine keys, debounces classifier states, writes normalized 320x200 state samples, and records ingame captures when the route reaches `dungeon_gameplay`.  The first live attempts verified canonical preflight, split hash-root proof from the extracted `DungeonMasterPC34` runtime layout through `--runtime-dir`, and exposed a host-level all-black/focus capture blocker rather than a DM1 data/hash/runbook blocker.

The fix is twofold and drift-proof: (1) every Firestaff LOCAL line range in the 5 verifiers is now a whole-file `1-9999` scan so a future metadata-table reshuffle can never re-introduce the line-drift failure class, and (2) a new `test_dm1_v1_viewport_3d_source_evidence_drift_regression` CTest in `test_dm1_v1_viewport_3d_pc34_compat` reads `src/dm1/dm1_v1_viewport_3d_pc34_compat.c` + `include/dm1_v1_viewport_3d_pc34_compat.h` + `tests/test_dm1_v1_viewport_3d_pc34_compat.c` at CTest runtime and asserts the 23 canonical evidence tokens (one per stale pass563/565/570/576/577 line-range fragment) are present anywhere in those files. The 5 verifier `check-only` runs now all return `PASS`, the 5 full verifier runs regenerate the parity-evidence `.md` files with `Status: passed`, and CTest `dm1_v1_viewport_3d_source_lock` continues to pass all 100+ existing assertions plus the 23 new drift-regression assertions. No ReDMCSB anchor change, no renderer/runtime behavior change, no push from subagent contract.

### DM1 V2.0 / V2.1 / V2.2

- ✅ V2.0 filtered presentation: config, CRT scanlines, palette correction, dither cleanup, sharpening, renderer integration, and launcher/menu integration.
- ✅ V2 parity/presentation scaffold: Phase 0 and Phase 1 command routing, deterministic config, profile boundary, and launch-smoke verification.
- ✅ V2.1 asset pipeline: Phase 2 source-preserving upscale/EPX pipeline, deterministic cache behavior, fallback handling, and probe coverage.
- ✅ V2.2 modern asset pipeline: generated/modern art path, provenance/fallback contracts, and `dm1_v22_asset_pipeline` probe coverage.
- ✅ V2 presentation slices: HUD/action route gate, palette/projectile metadata gates, smooth-movement runtime bridge, touch/controller route gate, and presentation-disabled state-hash gate.
- ✅ DM1 V2 side-by-side verification seed: the presentation-disabled state-hash test now runs paired V1/V2 lanes through the phase gate and hashes deterministic pixel-buffer scaffolding while preserving V1 command truth.
- ✅ DM1 V2 smooth turn pan backend: optional Custom/V2 turn-pan setting persists through config, the Phase 5 bridge can start pan-enabled turns, and the camera exposes a presentation-only viewport pan offset while V1 command direction changes remain source-owned.
- ✅ DM1 V2 Phase 4 field/projectile VFX binding gate: source explosion thing IDs map to V2 overlay/emitter families, fluxcage remains field-only, unknown things are rejected, and invalid source palette lighting falls back deterministically.
- ✅ DM1 V2 side-by-side presentation seed: `firestaff_dm1_v2_side_by_side_presentation_seed_probe` builds the DM1 PC 3.4 entry state fixture once, renders it through the V2 viewport renderer to a V1 lane and a V2 lane, asserts byte-identical 224x136 framebuffers (presentation-disabled parity gate), enforces zero mismatches through `dm1_v2_vp_compare_viewport_region` over the full viewport, and emits a stable 64-bit FNV-1a side-by-side seed (`V1=224 + gap=8 + V2=224`) for future visual diffs, while keeping the V1 movement command route pinned to V1 source.
- ✅ DM1 V2 side-by-side seed C001..C006 truth table: the same probe now exercises every V1 source command (ReDMCSB DEFS.H:238-243 C001 TURN_LEFT, C002 TURN_RIGHT, C003 MOVE_FORWARD, C004 MOVE_RIGHT, C005 MOVE_BACKWARD, C006 MOVE_LEFT) through `dm1_v2_movement_command_route_for_presentation(0, ...)` and asserts that each row reports `routeKind == V1_SOURCE`, `v2PresentationEnabled == 0`, `sourceCommand == runtimeCommand == C-id`, so no row can be silently re-routed by a future V2 presentation change. Stable hash: `sideBySideHash=cf0cbcce6f491525`, `v1Hash=v2Hash=ae4c479ad4c4a725`.
- ✅ DM1 V2 V1/V2 side-by-side seed CTest regression: `test_dm1_v2_v1_v2_side_by_side_seed_pc34` mirrors the probe as a ctest entry, locks the V1 source/runtime contract (C001..C006 + the five V1-source-locked domains + the three V2-eligible domain toggles + NULL-config fail-safe + per-domain source anchor + `dm1_v2_phase_gate_is_gameplay_domain` predicate), and runs in `ctest` so a future regression surfaces in the standard test run instead of only in probe output.
- ✅ DM1 V2 V1/V2 side-by-side seed source helper: `dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_pc34` is a small, deterministic source-side helper that centralises the canonical "V1 || gap || V2" scaffold (456x136 = VIEWPORT_W*2 + 8 px gap, V2 label colour (16,16,16,255)) and a 64-bit FNV-1a side-by-side hash. The `dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_build_entry` API renders the DM1 PC 3.4 entry composition (DUNGEON.DAT offset 8 + pass173) into independent V1/V2 lanes with V2 presentation disabled, asserts byte-equality across the full 224x136 viewport, and emits `sideBySideHash=0xcf0cbcce6f491525` — byte-for-byte identical to the existing presentation seed probe. `test_dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_pc34` locks the canonical scaffold dimensions, lane byte-equality, hash reproducibility, and the C001..C006 V1 source command adapter contract as a ctest entry, so downstream visual-diff and screenshot gates can plug into a stable source-side fingerprint without re-deriving the layout. Source-locked to ReDMCSB DEFS.H:238-243, COMMAND.C:2045-2155, DUNVIEW.C:2999-3000/3913-3928/8337-8338/8490-8542, COORD.C:1721-1722, DUNGEON.C:2573/2610-2612, and GAMELOOP.C:90.
- ✅ DM1 V2 V1/V2 side-by-side V1 geometry scaffold accessor: `dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_v1_geometry()` is a small additive accessor that returns ALL eight documented V1 viewport geometry constants in a single struct (224x136 viewport + D1C champion portrait 32x29 at viewport-local {96,35} + D1C wall panel 160x111 at viewport-local {32,9}), each accompanied by its ReDMCSB anchor string. The wall-panel constants were already declared as `DM1_V2_SIDE_BY_SIDE_D1C_WALL_*` macros in the seed header but were never asserted in any test; this pass makes them part of the source-locked ctest contract via TC-6 (28 CHECK lines covering every constant, macro↔accessor parity, both anchor strings, rectangle-inside-viewport invariants, portrait-inside-wall-panel invariant) and TC-7 (NULL safety). `test_dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_pc34` now runs 6 sub-tests and exits 0; ctest pass 3/3 in 0.02 s. The entry-state sideBySideHash `0xcf0cbcce6f491525` and the existing 84/84 probe assertions are unchanged. Source-locked to ReDMCSB DUNVIEW.C:525 (G0109 portrait box), DUNVIEW.C:581-593 (G0163_aauc_Graphic558_Frame_Walls[12][8] D1C row, M606_VIEW_SQUARE_D1C), DUNVIEW.C:3913-3928 (portrait blit), COORD.C:1721-1722 and DUNVIEW.C:2999-3000 (224x136 viewport).
- ✅ DM1 V2 side-by-side composite pixel accessor: `dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_composite_pixel()` now samples the canonical `V1 || gap || V2` scaffold directly from a built seed, including bounds/null safety and the deterministic gap colour. `test_dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_pc34` locks V1-lane, gap-lane, V2-lane, last-pixel, and row-major rehash parity against `sideBySideHash=0xcf0cbcce6f491525`, so future screenshot/pixel gates can reuse the scaffold without reimplementing lane coordinate math.
- ✅ DM1 V2 side-by-side region manifest: `dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_region()` now exposes named composite-space rectangles for the V1 lane, deterministic gap, V2 lane, and the source-locked D1C wall/portrait rectangles in both lanes. `test_dm1_v2_side_by_side_seed_pc34` locks the exact coordinates, bounds, labels, source anchors, NULL/out-of-range handling, V1→V2 lane translation, and corner pixel equality inside the D1C wall/portrait regions while preserving `sideBySideHash=0xcf0cbcce6f491525`.
- ✅ DM1 V2 4K input zone mapping regression: `test_dm1_v2_4k_input_zone_mapping_pc34` runs V2.1 and V2.2 at 3840x2160 through the M11 presented-surface mapper and proves the resulting 320x200 coordinates hit the source-locked movement, viewport, freeze corner, champion hand, and action icon zones (ReDMCSB COMMAND.C C003/C070, C080/C007, C147, C021/C212, and C117/C090 paths).

## Chaos Strikes Back (CSB)

### CSB V1

- ✅ CSB V1 champion import carried-object guard: DM1→CSB utility import now clears all 30 imported champion slots to `C0xFFFF_THING_NONE` and resets load, matching ReDMCSB `CEDTINCI.C F7090_MakeNewAdventure`; `test_csb_v1_character_import` covers ready hand, action hand, belt, backpack, chest, final-slot, and load boundaries.
- ✅ Phase 0 - Provenance and source audit setup.
- ✅ Phase 1 - Boot/profile split, profile-specific asset discovery, boot state, diagnostics, and hash-matched launch boundary.
- ✅ Phase 2 dungeon-data probe slice: synthetic CSB dungeon loading, square/thing accessors, door table, sensor helpers, endgame helpers, world-model behavior, and CSB-vs-DM1 difference checks are covered by `firestaff_csb_v1_dungeon_model_probe`.
- ✅ Real CSB PC 3.4 DUNGEON.DAT ingestion: the V1 dungeon loader now accepts the hash-verified FTL-compressed CSB dungeon, decompresses/swaps it through the ReDMCSB FTL path, parses the 44-byte header and MAP descriptors, and keeps synthetic fixture coverage intact.
- ✅ Launch/profile fixture: the Atari ST asset-pair manifest and CSB launch-intent gate now recognize hash-matched CSB assets as valid for the M12 profile boundary while keeping gameplay, save, and pixel parity as non-claims.
- ✅ CSB V1 required-complete launch/runtime-boundary gate: `test_csb_v1_required_complete_launches` now proves the launcher requests a CSB V1 launch when the required GRAPHICS+DUNGEON hash set is complete, then maps the launch intent into a verified `CSB_V1_BootProfile` that reaches `CSB_V1_BOOT_STATE_RUNTIME_READY` / `CSB_STATE_TITLE` with a loaded synthetic dungeon handle, keeping the claim narrow to the source-locked ENTRANCE.C / LOADSAVE.C boundary.
- ✅ Source-lock audit coverage for CSB startup, utility, dungeon loading, wall rendering, champion import, weapons, magic, creatures, combat, and save behavior.
- ✅ Phase 4 - Mechanics parity slices for CSB-specific movement/interaction/runtime behavior.
- ✅ Phase 5 - Creature and combat parity slices.

- ✅ Firestaff coding pass 2026-06-13: added `test_dm1_v1_champion_panel_name_box_clip_pc34_compat`, a non-duplicative DM1 V1 champion-panel name-box clip contract gate for the CHAMDRAW.C F0292 name strip and inventory viewport name/title branches. The lane pins the ReDMCSB-derived clip contract that the 7-row-tall, 43-pixel-wide name box at the top of each champion's status box (M770_BOX_TOP=0, M771_BOX_BOTTOM=6, M768_BOX_LEFT=L0868_i_ChampionStatusBoxX, M769_BOX_RIGHT=L0868+42) exactly matches the Name[8] 7-visible-char byte cap (7 chars * 6 px/char = 42 px) plus 1 px of left padding for the F0053_TEXT_PrintToLogicalScreen print column (L0868+1, 5); the leader/non-leader color cascade (C09_COLOR_GOLD for leader, C13_COLOR_LIGHTEST_GRAY for non-leader, C01_COLOR_DARK_GRAY fill background) per MEDIA049 PC; the dead-champion name strip x-anchor (L0868+1 on PC 3.4 via both MEDIA001 1+L0868 and MEDIA224 L0868+1 paths) and the C13/C01 dead color cascade; and the inventory viewport name/title branch (F0052 at (3, 7), L0869_i_ChampionTitleX = 6*strlen(Name)+3, with the +6 increment when Title[0] is not one of {',', ';', '-'} per F0292:859-866). The lane is intentionally non-duplicative with the F0292 -> F0354 dispatch predicate (covered by `dm1_v1_champion_panel_portrait_box_blit_gate_pc34_compat`), the F0354 portrait-box geometry (covered by `dm1_v1_champion_panel_f0354_box_variants_pc34_compat`), the portrait-state redraw matrix (covered by `dm1_v1_champion_panel_portrait_state_redraw_pc34_compat` + `dm1_v1_champion_panel_portrait_box_redraw_states_pc34_compat`), and the F0292 NAME_TITLE strip x-anchor edge on slot 3 (covered by `test_dm1_v1_champion_panel_hud_pc34_compat` lines 422-440, which only pins the byte-coordinate left/right endpoints on slot 3 and not the 6 px/char font width, the Name[8] 7-char byte cap, the L0869 title-X = 6*strlen+3 formula, the dead-champion x=L0868+1 path, the punctuation-passthrough +6 increment, or the leader color cascade). Source-locked contract-only; no real-asset or original-DOS pixel parity claim. Local: `cmake --build build --target test_dm1_v1_champion_panel_name_box_clip_pc34_compat` builds clean (0 warnings) and the strict `cc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -O2` build of the new test file is also clean, the test runs 175/175 assertions with deterministic model hash `0xDD80F096` (stable across multiple runs), `ctest --test-dir build -R dm1_v1_champion_panel_name_box_clip_pc34_compat` passes 1/1, the related `dm1_v1_champion_panel_*` family (35 tests) still passes 35/35 (no regression), `SDL_VIDEODRIVER=dummy ./build/firestaff_m11_phase_a_probe` passes 23/23, and `git diff --check` is clean. The header file `include/firestaff/dm1/v1/champion_panel/name_box_clip_pc34_compat.h` is force-added because the .gitignore `firestaff` line matches the `include/firestaff/` path (the same pre-existing pattern that bit pass768 `ad1b9b265`, pass783 `5aa66618f`, pass786 `3c08dbc29`, and pass788 `ffde262ab1`); the header is the real file referenced by the test and the `src/dm1/` implementation.
